\left[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{*})\right](s_{h,j},a_{h,j})\leq E_{h,j}
and
\left[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}(\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{*})\right](s_{h,j},a_{h,j})\leq E_{h,j}
for all
j\leq k\leq K
with
E_{h,j}
defined in (
4.7
).
As a result, for all
j\leq k
,

\displaystyle\sigma_{h,j}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq d^{3}H\cdot E_{h,j}\geq d^{3}H\cdot\max\left\{\left[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{*})\right](s_{h,j},a_{h,j}),\left[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}(\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{*})\right](s_{h,j},a_{h,j})\right\}.

It implies
C_{\sigma}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{d^{3}H}}
and for any
j\in[k]
,

{\mathcal{A}}_{h,j}\subseteq\left\{\sigma_{h,j}^{2}\geq C_{\sigma}^{-2}\max\left\{\left[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{*})\right](s_{h,j},a_{h,j}),\left[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}(\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{*})\right](s_{h,j},a_{h,j})\right\}\right\}.

Finally, with by Lemma
F.8
and
F.7
, the covering entropy for
\varepsilon_{0}=\min\left\{\frac{\sigma_{\min}}{\sqrt{d^{3}H}},\frac{\lambda{\mathcal{H}}\sqrt{d}}{K}\sigma_{\min}^{2}\right\}
and the function class to which
\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{*}
and
\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{*}
belong is

\displaystyle\log N_{0}

\displaystyle=|{\mathcal{N}}({\mathcal{V}}^{\pm},\varepsilon_{0})|\leq\left[d\log\left(1+\frac{4L}{\varepsilon_{0}}\right)+d^{2}\log\left(1+\frac{8\sqrt{d}B^{2}}{\lambda\varepsilon_{0}^{2}}\right)\right]\cdot dH\log_{2}\left(1+\frac{K}{\lambda\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle={\mathcal{O}}(d^{3}H\iota_{1}^{2})

By Lemma
E.1
, with probability at least
1-\frac{\delta}{H}
,

\max\left\{\left\|({\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{h,k-1})(\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k}-{\bm{V}}_{h+1}^{*})\right\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}},\left\|({\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{h,k-1})(\underline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k}-{\bm{V}}_{h+1}^{*})\right\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}}\right\}\leq\beta_{2}

for all
k\in[K]
with
\beta_{2}
defined in the following.
Finally, we simplify
\beta_{2}
as

\displaystyle\beta_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{8}{\sqrt{d^{3}H}}\sqrt{d\log\left(1+\frac{K}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}d\lambda}\right)\log\frac{4N_{0}HK^{2}}{\delta}}+\frac{8}{d^{2.5}H}\log\frac{4N_{0}HK^{2}}{\delta}+\sqrt{d\lambda}{\mathcal{H}}

\displaystyle\leq 8\sqrt{\frac{\iota_{1}}{d^{2}H}\cdot\left(\iota_{1}+{\mathcal{O}}(d^{3}H\iota_{1}^{2})\right)}+\frac{8}{d^{2.5}H}\left(\iota_{1}+{\mathcal{O}}(d^{3}H\iota_{1}^{2})\right)+\sqrt{d\lambda}{\mathcal{H}}

\displaystyle={\mathcal{O}}\left(\sqrt{d}\iota_{1}^{1.5}+\iota_{1}+\sqrt{d}\iota_{1}^{2}+\sqrt{d\lambda}{\mathcal{H}}\right)={\mathcal{O}}\left(\sqrt{d}\iota_{1}^{2}+\sqrt{d\lambda}{\mathcal{H}}\right).

Putting pieces together

we have shown that given
{\mathcal{B}}_{h+1}\cap{\mathcal{B}}_{0}
is true, with probability at least
1-2\delta
, for all
h\in[H]
and
k\in[K]
,

\max\left\{\left\|({\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{h,k-1})\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k}\right\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}},\left\|({\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*}-{\bm{\mu}}_{h,k-1})\underline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k}\right\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}}\right\}\leq\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}={\mathcal{O}}\left(\sqrt{d}\iota_{1}^{2}+\sqrt{d\lambda}{\mathcal{H}}\right).

Therefore,
{\mathcal{B}}_{V}:={\mathcal{B}}_{1}
holds.
∎

E.9
Proof of Lemma
C.8

Proof of Lemma
C.8
.

For a given
k
, let
k_{\mathrm{last}}
denote the latest update episode before episode
k
, that is
k_{\mathrm{last}}\leq k<k_{\mathrm{last}}+1
.
By Lemma
F.6
, due to
{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}\succeq{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}
and
\det({\bm{H}}_{h,k-1})\leq 2\det({\bm{H}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1})
, it follows that for any
{\bm{x}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
,

\|{\bm{x}}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}^{-1}}\leq 2\|{\bm{x}}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}.

(E.5)

By definition,
\overline{Q}_{h}^{k}(\cdot,\cdot)\leq\langle{\bm{\phi}}(\cdot,\cdot),{\bm{\theta}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}+{\bm{\mu}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}\rangle+\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}^{-1}}
and
Q_{h}^{\pi_{k}}(s,a)=\langle{\bm{\phi}}(s,a),{\bm{\theta}}_{h}^{*}+{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*}{\bm{V}}_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}\rangle
.
Using
a_{h,k}=\pi_{h}^{k}(s_{h,k})=\mathop{\rm argmax}_{a\in{\mathcal{A}}}\overline{Q}_{h}^{k}(s_{h,k},a)
, we then have

\displaystyle(

\displaystyle\overline{V}_{h}^{k}-V_{h}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k})\leq(\overline{Q}_{h}^{k}-Q_{h}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

\displaystyle\leq\langle{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k},{\bm{\theta}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}+{\bm{\mu}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}-({\bm{\theta}}_{h}^{*}+{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*}{\bm{V}}_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})\rangle+\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\langle{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k},({\bm{\theta}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{h}^{*})+({\bm{\mu}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}-{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*})\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}\rangle+\langle{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k},{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*}(\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}-{\bm{V}}_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})\rangle+2\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}4\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+\langle{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k},{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*}(\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k}-{\bm{V}}_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}4\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{=}4\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h+1,k})+X_{h,k}.

Here
(a)
uses (
E.5
),
(b)
uses

\displaystyle|\langle{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k},({\bm{\theta}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{h}^{*})+({\bm{\mu}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}-{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*})\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}^{-1}}\|({\bm{\theta}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}-{\bm{\theta}}_{h}^{*})+({\bm{\mu}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}-{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*})\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}^{-1}}\leq 2\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}

on
{\mathcal{B}}_{R}\cap{\mathcal{B}}_{V}
,
(c)
uses
\langle{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k},{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})\rangle={\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}-{\bm{V}}_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})={\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k}-{\bm{V}}_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})
, and
(d)
uses the notation

X_{h,k}:={\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})-(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h+1,k}).

The last inequality implies

\displaystyle(\overline{V}_{h}^{k}-V_{h}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k})\leq(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h+1,k})+X_{h,k}+4\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}.

Iterating the above inequality over
h
and using
\overline{V}_{H+1}^{k}(\cdot)=V_{H+1}^{\pi_{k}}(\cdot)=0
, we have

(\overline{V}_{h}^{k}-V_{h}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k})\leq\sum_{i=h}^{H}\left[X_{i,k}+4\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{i,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{i,k-1}^{-1}}\right].

(E.6)

Therefore, setting
h=1
and summing (
E.6
) over
k\in[K]
, we have

\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\overline{V}_{1}^{k}-V_{1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{1,k})\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[X_{h,k}+4\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}\right].

(E.7)

We then need to analyze
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{h,k}
.
Since
s_{h+1,k}
is
{\mathcal{F}}_{h+1,k}
-measurable,
\pi_{k}=\{\pi_{h}^{k}\}_{h\in[H]},\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}
is
{\mathcal{F}}_{H,k-1}
-measurable, we have
X_{h,k}
is
{\mathcal{F}}_{h+1,k}
-measurable.
We also have
{\mathbb{E}}[X_{h,k}|{\mathcal{F}}_{h,k}]=0
,
|X_{h,k}|\leq 2{\mathcal{H}}
and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[X_{h,k}^{2}|{\mathcal{F}}_{h,k}]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}[(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})^{2}(s_{h+1,k})|{\mathcal{F}}_{h,k}]\overset{(a)}{\leq}{\mathcal{H}}{\mathbb{E}}[|\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}|(s_{h+1,k})|{\mathcal{F}}_{h,k}]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}{\mathcal{H}}{\mathbb{E}}[(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h+1,k})|{\mathcal{F}}_{h,k}]={\mathcal{H}}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

where
(a)
uses
|\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}|(\cdot)\leq{\mathcal{H}}
and
(b)
uses the optimism in Lemma
C.4
.
By the variance-aware Freedman inequality in Lemma
F.2
, with probability at least
1-\frac{\delta}{2}
, it follows that

\left|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{h,k}\right|\leq 3\sqrt{\iota}\cdot\sqrt{{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})}+10{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\iota

(E.8)

where
\iota=\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}
.
On the other hand, it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}(\overline{V}_{h}^{k}-V_{h}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k})+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{h,k}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}\sum_{i=h}^{H}\left[X_{i,k}+4\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{i,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{i,k-1}^{-1}}\right]+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{h,k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}(H-h+1)\left[X_{h,k}+4\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}\right]+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{h,k}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}4H\beta\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{h,k}b_{h}

where
(a)
uses (
E.6
) and
(b)
uses the notation
b_{h}=1
if
h=1
; otherwise
=H-h+2
for
2\leq h\leq H
.
Clearly, we have
|b_{h}|\leq H
for all
h\in[H]
.
By the variance-aware Freedman inequality in Lemma
F.2
, with probability at least
1-\frac{\delta}{2}
, it follows that

\left|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{h,k}b_{h}\right|\leq 3H\sqrt{\iota}\cdot\sqrt{{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})}+10H{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\iota.

As a result, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

\displaystyle\leq 3H\sqrt{\iota}\cdot\sqrt{{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})}

\displaystyle\qquad+4H\beta\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+10H{\mathcal{H}}\iota.

Using the inequality that
x\leq 2(a^{2}+b^{2})
for any
x\leq|a|\sqrt{x}+b^{2}
, we have

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})\leq 8H\beta\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+38H^{2}{\mathcal{H}}\iota.

(E.9)

Putting pieces together, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}

\displaystyle(\overline{V}_{1}^{k}-V_{1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{1,k})\overset{\eqref{eq:final0}}{\leq}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[X_{h,k}+4\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:final1}}{\leq}4\beta\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+3\sqrt{\iota}\cdot\sqrt{{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})}+10{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\iota

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:final-expect-sum}}{\leq}4\beta\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+3\sqrt{\iota}\cdot\sqrt{{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\left[8H\beta\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+38H^{2}{\mathcal{H}}\iota\right]}+10{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\iota

\displaystyle\leq 6\beta\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+38H{\mathcal{H}}\iota

where the last inequality uses
\sqrt{a+b}\leq\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}
and
2\sqrt{ab}\leq a+b
for non-negative numbers
a,b\geq 0
.
∎

E.10
Proof of Lemma
C.9

Proof of Lemma
C.9
.

The proof main idea is similar to that in Lemma
C.8
.
For a given
k
, let
k_{\mathrm{last}}
denote the latest update episode before episode
k
, that is
k_{\mathrm{last}}\leq k<k_{\mathrm{last}}+1
.
By definition,
\overline{Q}_{h}^{k}(\cdot,\cdot)\leq\langle{\bm{\phi}}(\cdot,\cdot),{\bm{\theta}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}+{\bm{\mu}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}\rangle+\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}^{-1}}
and
\underline{Q}_{h}^{k}(\cdot,\cdot)\geq\langle{\bm{\phi}}(\cdot,\cdot),{\bm{\theta}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}+{\bm{\mu}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}\underline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}\rangle-\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}(\cdot,\cdot)\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}^{-1}}
.
Using

a_{h,k}=\pi_{h}^{k}(s_{h,k})=\mathop{\rm argmax}_{a\in{\mathcal{A}}}\overline{Q}_{h}^{k}(s_{h,k},a),

we then have

\displaystyle(

\displaystyle\overline{V}_{h}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h}^{k})(s_{h,k})\leq(\overline{Q}_{h}^{k}-\underline{Q}_{h}^{k})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

\displaystyle\leq\langle{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k},{\bm{\mu}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}(\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}-\underline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}})\rangle+2\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\langle{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k},({\bm{\mu}}_{h,k-1}-{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*})(\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}-\underline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}})\rangle+\langle{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k},{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*}(\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}-\underline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}})\rangle+4\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}6\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+\langle{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k},{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*}(\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}-\underline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}})\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}6\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{=}6\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})(s_{h+1,k})+X_{h,k}.

Here
(a)
uses (
E.5
),
(b)
uses

\displaystyle|\langle{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k},({\bm{\mu}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}-{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*})(\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}-\underline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}})\rangle|

\displaystyle\leq\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}^{-1}}\|({\bm{\mu}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}-{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*})(\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}-\underline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}})\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{\mathrm{last}}-1}^{-1}}\leq 2\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}

on
{\mathcal{B}}_{V}\cap{\mathcal{B}}_{R}
,
(c)
uses
\langle{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k},{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}})\rangle={\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k_{\mathrm{last}}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})={\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})
, and
(d)
uses the notation

X_{h,k}:={\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})-(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})(s_{h+1,k}).

(E.10)

The last inequality implies

\displaystyle(\overline{V}_{h}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h}^{k})(s_{h,k})\leq(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})(s_{h+1,k})+X_{h,k}+6\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}.

Iterating the above inequality over
h
and using
\overline{V}_{H+1}^{k}(\cdot)=\underline{V}_{H+1}^{k}(\cdot)=0
, we have

(\overline{V}_{h}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h}^{k})(s_{h,k})\leq\sum_{i=h}^{H}\left[X_{i,k}+6\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{i,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{i,k-1}^{-1}}\right].

(E.11)

Using the last inequality, it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}(\overline{V}_{h}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h}^{k})(s_{h,k})+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{h,k}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}\sum_{i=h}^{H}\left[X_{i,k}+8\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{i,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{i,k-1}^{-1}}\right]+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{h,k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}(H-h+1)\left[X_{h,k}+6\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}\right]+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{h,k}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}6H\beta\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{h,k}b_{h}

(E.12)

where
(a)
uses (
E.6
) and
(b)
uses the notation
b_{h}=1
if
h=1
; otherwise
=H-h+2
for
2\leq h\leq H
.
Clearly, we have
|b_{h}|\leq H
for all
h\in[H]
.

We then need to analyze
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{h,k}b_{h}
with
X_{h,k}
’s defined in (
E.10
).
Since
s_{h+1,k}
is
{\mathcal{F}}_{h+1,k}
-measurable,
\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k},\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}
is
{\mathcal{F}}_{H,k-1}
-measurable, we have
X_{h,k}
is
{\mathcal{F}}_{h+1,k}
-measurable.
We also have
{\mathbb{E}}[X_{h,k}|{\mathcal{F}}_{h,k}]=0
,
|X_{h,k}|\leq 2{\mathcal{H}}
and

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[X_{h,k}^{2}|{\mathcal{F}}_{h,k}]

\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}[(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})^{2}(s_{h+1,k})|{\mathcal{F}}_{h,k}]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{\mathcal{H}}{\mathbb{E}}[|\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}|(s_{h+1,k})|{\mathcal{F}}_{h,k}]={\mathcal{H}}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

where
(a)
uses
|\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}|(\cdot)\leq{\mathcal{H}}
.
By the variance-aware Freedman inequality in Lemma
F.2
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, it follows that

\left|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}X_{h,k}b_{h}\right|\leq 3H\sqrt{\iota}\cdot\sqrt{{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})}+10H{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\iota

(E.13)

where
\iota=\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}
.
As a result, plugging (
E.13
) into (
E.10
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

\displaystyle\leq 3H\sqrt{\iota}\cdot\sqrt{{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})}

\displaystyle\qquad+6H\beta\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=2}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+10H{\mathcal{H}}\iota.

Using the inequality that
x\leq 2(a^{2}+b^{2})
for any
x\leq|a|\sqrt{x}+b^{2}
, we have

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})\leq 12H\beta\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+38H^{2}{\mathcal{H}}\iota.

∎

E.11
Proof of Lemma
C.10

Proof of Lemma
C.10
.

Recall that
b_{h,k}=\max\{\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}},\|\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}_{h,k}\|_{\widetilde{{\bm{H}}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}\},w_{h,k}=\sigma_{h,k}^{-1}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}
and
\widetilde{w}_{h,k}=\sigma_{h,k}^{-1}\|\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}_{h,k}\|_{\widetilde{{\bm{H}}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}
.
As a result, we have
\sigma_{h,k}^{-1}b_{h,k}=\max\left\{w_{h,k},\widetilde{w}_{h,k}\right\}
.
On the other hand,

\sigma_{h,k}^{2}=\max\left\{\sigma_{\min}^{2},d^{3}H\cdot E_{h,k},J_{h,k},c_{0}^{-2}b_{h,k}^{2},\left(\frac{W}{\sqrt{c_{1}d}}+{\mathcal{H}}d^{2.5}H\right)b_{h,k}\right\}.

(
4.5
)

Based on what value
\sigma_{h,k}
takes, we compose the full index set
{\mathcal{I}}:=[H]\times[K]
into three disjoint sets with ties broken arbitrarily:

\displaystyle{\mathcal{J}}_{1}=\left\{(h,k)\subseteq[H]\times[K]:\sigma_{h,k}^{2}\in\left\{\sigma_{\min}^{2},d^{3}H\cdot E_{h,k},U_{h,k}\right\}\right\},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{J}}_{2}=\left\{(h,k)\subseteq[H]\times[K]:\sigma_{h,k}^{2}=c_{0}^{-2}b_{h,k}^{2}\right\},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{J}}_{3}=\left\{(h,k)\subseteq[H]\times[K]:\sigma_{h,k}^{2}=\left(\frac{W}{\sqrt{c_{1}d}}+{\mathcal{H}}d^{2.5}H\right)b_{h,k}\right\}.

For simplicity, we denote
z_{h,k}:=\frac{b_{h,k}}{\sigma_{h,k}}=\max\{w_{h,k},\widetilde{w}_{h,k}\}
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}b_{h,k}=\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}\sigma_{h,k}z_{h,k}=\sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{i}}\sigma_{h,k}z_{h,k}.

(E.14)

Recall that
\kappa=d\log\left(1+\frac{K}{d\lambda\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)
, we have
\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}z_{h,k}^{2}\leq 4H\kappa
.
This is because

\displaystyle\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}z_{h,k}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}\left(w_{h,k}^{2}+\widetilde{w}_{h,k}^{2}\right)\overset{(a)}{=}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\min\left\{1,w_{h,k}^{2}\right\}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\min\left\{1,\widetilde{w}_{h,k}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}4Hd\log\left(1+\frac{K}{d\lambda\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)=4H\kappa.

where
(a)
uses
z_{h,k}\leq c_{0}\leq 1
due to
\sigma_{h,k}\geq c_{0}^{-1}b_{h,k},c_{0}\leq 1
and
(b)
uses Lemma
F.5
.
We will frequently use the above inequality.

Now, we are ready to analyze the three terms in the RHS of (
E.14
) respectively.

•

For the first term, it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{1}}\sigma_{h,k}z_{h,k}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{1}}\sigma_{h,k}^{2}}\sqrt{\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{1}}z_{h,k}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{1}}(\sigma_{\min}^{2}+d^{3}H\cdot E_{h,k}+J_{h,k})}\sqrt{\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{1}}z_{h,k}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}(\sigma_{\min}^{2}+d^{3}H\cdot E_{h,k}+J_{h,k})}\sqrt{\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}z_{h,k}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{HK\sigma_{\min}^{2}+\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}(d^{3}H\cdot E_{h,k}+J_{h,k})}\cdot\sqrt{4H\kappa}.

We provide a upper bound for
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}E_{h,k}
in Lemma
E.2
whose proof is deferred in Appendix
E.11.1
.

Lemma E.2

(Sum of
E_{h,k}
)
.

On the event
{\mathcal{B}}_{0}\cap{\mathcal{A}}_{0}
,

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}E_{h,k}={\mathcal{O}}\left((\beta_{0}+H\beta){\mathcal{H}}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+H^{2}{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}\right).

where
{\mathcal{O}}(\cdot)
hides universal positive constants.

We also provide a upper bound for
\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}J_{h,k}
in Lemma
E.3
whose proof is deferred in Appendix
E.11.2
.

Lemma E.3

(Sum of
J_{h,k}
)
.

Recall that
J_{h,k}=[\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{h}R_{h}+\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})+R_{h,k}+U_{h,k}
with
R_{h,k},U_{h,k}
defined in (
4.8
) and (
4.9
) respectively.
On the event
{\mathcal{B}}_{R}\cap{\mathcal{B}}_{V}\cap{\mathcal{B}}_{0}\cap{\mathcal{A}}_{0}
, with probability at least
1-2\delta
,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}J_{h,k}={\mathcal{O}}\left({\mathcal{G}}^{*}K+[(\beta_{0}+H\beta){\mathcal{H}}+\beta_{R^{2}}]\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}b_{h,k}+H^{2}{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}+H\sigma_{R}^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}\right).

where
{\mathcal{G}}^{*}
is defined in (
4.11
) and
{\mathcal{O}}(\cdot)
hides universal positive constants.

Putting pieces together and using
\sqrt{a+b+c}\leq\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}+\sqrt{c}
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{1}}b_{h,k}&=\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{1}}\sigma_{h,k}z_{h,k}={\mathcal{O}}\left(\sqrt{H\kappa}\cdot\sqrt{K\left(H\sigma_{\min}^{2}+{\mathcal{G}}^{*}\right)}\right)\\
&\quad+{\mathcal{O}}\left(\sqrt{H\kappa}\cdot\sqrt{H^{3}d^{3}{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}+H\sigma_{R}^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}.}\right)\\
&\quad+{\mathcal{O}}\left(\sqrt{H\kappa}\cdot\sqrt{[(\beta_{0}+H\beta){\mathcal{H}}d^{3}H+\beta_{R^{2}}]\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}b_{h,k}}\right).\end{split}

(E.15)

•

For the second term, due to
\sigma_{h,k}=c_{0}^{-1}b_{h,k}
, we have
z_{h,k}=b_{h,k}/\sigma_{h,k}=c_{0}\leq 1
for all
(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{2}
.
Hence,

\displaystyle\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{2}}b_{h,k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{2}}\sigma_{h,k}z_{h,k}=\frac{1}{c_{0}}\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{2}}\sigma_{h,k}z_{h,k}^{2}\leq\frac{\sup_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}\sigma_{h,k}}{c_{0}}\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{2}}z_{h,k}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}\frac{\max\{\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}},\|\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}_{h,k}\|_{\widetilde{{\bm{H}}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}\}}{c_{0}^{2}}\cdot\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}z_{h,k}^{2}\leq\frac{4H\kappa}{c_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\lambda}}

(E.16)

where the last inequality uses
\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}}
and
\|\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}_{h,k}\|_{\widetilde{{\bm{H}}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}}\|\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}_{h,k}\|\leq\frac{1}{\sqrt{\lambda}}
for any
(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}
.

•

For the third term,
\sigma_{h,k}^{2}=\left(\frac{W}{\sqrt{c_{1}d}}+{\mathcal{H}}d^{2.5}H\right)b_{h,k}
and thus
\sigma_{h,k}=\left(\frac{W}{\sqrt{c_{1}d}}+{\mathcal{H}}d^{2.5}H\right)z_{h,k}
.
Hence,

\displaystyle\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{3}}b_{h,k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{3}}\sigma_{h,k}z_{h,k}=\left(\frac{W}{\sqrt{c_{1}d}}+{\mathcal{H}}d^{2.5}H\right)\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{J}}_{3}}z_{h,k}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{W}{\sqrt{c_{1}d}}+{\mathcal{H}}d^{2.5}H\right)\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}z_{h,k}^{2}\leq 4H\kappa\cdot\left(\frac{W}{\sqrt{c_{1}d}}+{\mathcal{H}}d^{2.5}H\right).

(E.17)

Combing (
E.15
), (
•
‣
E.11
) and (
•
‣
E.11
), we have

\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}b_{h,k}={\mathcal{O}}\left(C+\sqrt{H\kappa}\sqrt{[(\beta_{0}+H\beta){\mathcal{H}}d^{3}H+\beta_{R^{2}}]\cdot\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}b_{h,k}}\right)

where

\displaystyle C

\displaystyle=\sqrt{H\kappa}\cdot\sqrt{K\left(H\sigma_{\min}^{2}+{\mathcal{G}}^{*}\right)}+H\kappa\cdot\left(\frac{W}{\sqrt{c_{1}d}}+\frac{1}{c_{0}^{2}\sqrt{\lambda}}+{\mathcal{H}}d^{2.5}H\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{H\kappa}\cdot\sqrt{H^{3}d^{3}{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}+H\sigma_{R}^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}}.

Using the inequality that
x\leq 2(a^{2}+b^{2})
for any
x\leq|a|\sqrt{x}+b^{2}
, we have

\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}b_{h,k}={\mathcal{O}}\left(C+H^{2}{\mathcal{H}}\kappa d^{3}\left(\beta_{0}+H\beta\right)+H\kappa\beta_{R^{2}}\right).

In the following, we are going to simplify the last inequality.
We will use
\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\cdot)
to hide logarithmic factors for simplicity.
Notice that
\kappa=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(d)
.
By setting
\lambda=\frac{1}{{\mathcal{H}}^{2}+W^{2}}
, we have
\beta_{R}=\beta_{V}=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{d})
and thus
\beta=\beta_{V}+\beta_{R}=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{d})
.
Moreover,
\beta_{R^{2}}=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{d}\frac{\sigma_{R^{2}}}{\sigma_{\min}}+\sqrt{\lambda}W\right)=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{d}\frac{\sigma_{R^{2}}}{\sigma_{\min}}\right)
and
\beta_{0}=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d^{3}H}{\mathcal{H}}}{\sigma_{\min}}+\sqrt{d\lambda}{\mathcal{H}}\right)=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{\sqrt{d^{3}H}{\mathcal{H}}}{\sigma_{\min}}+\sqrt{d}\right)
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}b_{h,k}

\displaystyle={\mathcal{O}}\left(C+H^{2}{\mathcal{H}}\kappa d^{3}\left(\beta_{0}+H\beta\right)+H\kappa\beta_{R^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle={\mathcal{O}}(C)+\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\frac{H^{2.5}d^{5.5}{\mathcal{H}}^{2}+Hd^{1.5}\sigma_{R^{2}}}{\sigma_{\min}}+H^{3}d^{4.5}{\mathcal{H}}+Hd^{1.5}\right).

We then analyze
C
.
Using
\sqrt{a+b}\leq\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}
for non-negative numbers
a,b\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle C=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\sqrt{dHK{\mathcal{G}}^{*}}+Hd^{0.5}K^{0.5}\sigma_{\min}+H^{2}d^{3.5}{\mathcal{H}}+H^{2}d^{2}{\mathcal{H}}+Hd^{0.5}\sigma_{R}+Hd\right).

Putting the results together, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{(h,k)\in{\mathcal{I}}}b_{h,k}=\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}\left(\sqrt{dHK{\mathcal{G}}^{*}}+Hd^{0.5}K^{0.5}\sigma_{\min}+\frac{H^{2.5}d^{5.5}{\mathcal{H}}^{2}+Hd^{1.5}\sigma_{R^{2}}}{\sigma_{\min}}+H^{3}d^{4.5}{\mathcal{H}}+Hd^{0.5}\sigma_{R}+Hd^{1.5}\right).

∎

E.11.1
Proof of Lemma
E.2

Proof of Lemma
E.2
.

By the definition of
E_{h,k}
in (
4.7
), it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}E_{h,k}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[2{\mathcal{H}}\beta_{0}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\left[\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{h,k}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})\right](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[4{\mathcal{H}}\beta_{0}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\left[{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})\right](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}(4\beta_{0}+16H\beta){\mathcal{H}}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+38H^{2}{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}

\displaystyle={\mathcal{O}}\left((\beta_{0}+H\beta){\mathcal{H}}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+H^{2}{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}\right)

where
(a)
uses
\left|[(\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{h,k}-{\mathbb{P}}_{h})\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})\right|=|\langle{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k},({\bm{\mu}}_{h,k-1}-{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*})\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}\rangle|\leq\beta_{0}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}
on
{\mathcal{B}}_{0}
and
(b)
follows from Lemma
C.9
.
∎

E.11.2
Proof of Lemma
E.3

Proof of Lemma
E.3
.

By Lemma
C.3
, on the event
{\mathcal{B}}_{R}
, we have
\left|[\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{h}\widehat{R}_{h}-{\mathbb{V}}_{h}R_{h}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})\right|\leq R_{h,k}
for all
h\in[H]
and
k\in[K]
.
By Lemma
C.5
, on the event
{\mathcal{B}}_{0}\cap{\mathcal{B}}_{V}
,
[\widehat{\mathbb{V}}_{h}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})\leq[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})+U_{h,k}
for all
h\in[H]
and
k\in[K]
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}J_{h,k}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}R_{h}+{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})+2\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}R_{h,k}+2\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}U_{h,k}

\displaystyle:=(I)+(II)+(III).

For the term
(III)
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}U_{h,k}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[11{\mathcal{H}}\beta_{0}\cdot\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+4{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{h,k}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[19{\mathcal{H}}\beta_{0}\cdot\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+4{\mathcal{H}}\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}(19\beta_{0}+64H\beta){\mathcal{H}}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+152H^{2}{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta},

(E.18)

where
(a)
uses
\left|[(\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{h,k}-{\mathbb{P}}_{h})\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})\right|=|\langle{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k},({\bm{\mu}}_{h,k-1}-{\bm{\mu}}_{h}^{*})\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}\rangle|\leq\beta_{0}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}
on
{\mathcal{B}}_{0}
; and
(b)
follows from Lemma
C.9
.

For the term
(II)
, we have

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}R_{h,k}=\beta_{R^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}_{h,k}\|_{\widetilde{{\bm{H}}}_{h,k-1}^{k-1}}+2{\mathcal{H}}\beta_{R}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}.

(E.19)

We provide two ways to analyze the term
(I)
.

•

On one hand, we denote
X_{k}=\sum_{h=1}^{H}[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}R_{h}+{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})
for simplicity.
Let
{\mathcal{G}}_{k}:={\mathcal{F}}_{H,k}
be the
\sigma
-field generated by all the random variables over the first
k
episodes.
Then
\pi_{k}
is
{\mathcal{G}}_{k-1}
-measurable,
X_{k}\geq 0
is
{\mathcal{G}}_{k}
-measurable, and
|X_{k}|\leq H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})
.
Therefore,
|X_{k}-{\mathbb{E}}[X_{k}|{\mathcal{G}}_{k-1}]|\leq H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})
and
\Var[X_{k}|{\mathcal{G}}_{k-1}]\leq H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\cdot{\mathbb{E}}[X_{k}|{\mathcal{G}}_{k-1}]
.
By the variance-aware Freedman inequality in Lemma
F.2
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}X_{k}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}[X_{k}|{\mathcal{F}}_{k-1}]+3\sqrt{H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}[X_{k}|{\mathcal{G}}_{k-1}]\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}}

\displaystyle\qquad+5H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq 3\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}[X_{k}|{\mathcal{F}}_{k-1}]+7H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}.

Notice that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[X_{k}|{\mathcal{F}}_{k-1}]

\displaystyle={\mathbb{E}}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}R_{h}+{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})\bigg|{\mathcal{G}}_{k-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{(s,a)}d_{h}^{\pi_{k}}(s,a)[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}R_{h}+{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}](s,a)

where
d_{h}^{\pi_{k}}(s,a)={\mathbb{P}}^{\pi_{k}}(s_{h}=s,a_{h}=a|s_{0}=s_{1,k})
is the probability reaching
(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})=(s,a)
at the
h
-th step when the agent starts from
s_{1,k}
and follows the policy
\pi_{k}
.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle(I)

\displaystyle\leq 3\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{(s,a)}d_{h}^{\pi_{k}}(s,a)[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}R_{h}+{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}](s,a)+7H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq 3{\mathcal{G}}_{0}^{*}K+7H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\log\frac{2\lceil\log_{2}K\rceil}{\delta}

where

{\mathcal{G}}_{0}^{*}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{(s,a)}d_{h}^{\pi_{k}}(s,a)[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}R_{h}+{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}](s,a).

•

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle(I)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}-{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}\right](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}R_{h}+{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{eq:var-diff}}{\leq}2{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}R_{h}+{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathcal{H}}\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})+2{\mathcal{V}}^{2}K+2H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\log\frac{1}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathcal{V}}^{2}K+2H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\log\frac{1}{\delta}+16H\beta{\mathcal{H}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+76H^{2}{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathcal{V}}^{2}K+16H\beta{\mathcal{H}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}+78H^{2}{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}+2H\sigma_{R}^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}

where the first inequality uses (
•
‣
E.11.2
), the second inequality uses Lemma
E.4
, and the third inequality uses Lemma
C.8
.

\displaystyle\left[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}-{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}\right](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

\displaystyle={\mathbb{P}}_{h}[V_{h+1}^{*}]^{2}(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})-[{\mathbb{P}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{*}(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})]^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\left({\mathbb{P}}_{h}[V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}]^{2}(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})-[{\mathbb{P}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})]^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}{\mathbb{P}}_{h}[V_{h+1}^{*}]^{2}(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})-{\mathbb{P}}_{h}[V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}]^{2}(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}2{\mathcal{H}}\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(V_{h+1}^{*}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}2{\mathcal{H}}\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}})(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

(E.20)

where
(a)
uses
V_{h+1}^{*}(\cdot)\geq V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}(\cdot)
,
(b)
uses
V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}(\cdot)\leq V_{h+1}^{*}(\cdot)\leq{\mathcal{H}}
, and
(c)
uses Lemma
C.4
.

Finally, we are going to put pieces together.
In order to simplicity notation, we use
b_{h,k}=\max\{\|{\bm{\phi}}_{h,k}\|_{{\bm{H}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}},\|\widetilde{{\bm{\phi}}}_{h,k}\|_{\widetilde{{\bm{H}}}_{h,k-1}^{-1}}\}
and
\beta=\beta_{V}+\beta_{R}
.
From the first bullet point, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}J_{h,k}={\mathcal{O}}\left({\mathcal{G}}_{0}^{*}\cdot K+[(\beta_{0}+H\beta){\mathcal{H}}+\beta_{R^{2}}]\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}b_{h,k}+H^{2}{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}+H\sigma_{R}^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}\right).

From the second bullet point, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}J_{h,k}={\mathcal{O}}\left({\mathcal{V}}^{2}K+[(\beta_{0}+H\beta){\mathcal{H}}+\beta_{R^{2}}]\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}b_{h,k}+H^{2}{\mathcal{H}}^{2}\log\frac{4\lceil\log_{2}HK\rceil}{\delta}+H\sigma_{R}^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}\right).

Taking minimum of the last two inequalities and using
\min\left\{{\mathcal{G}}_{0}^{*},{\mathcal{V}}^{2}\right\}\leq{\mathcal{G}}^{*}
complete the proof.
∎

E.11.3
Proof of Lemma
E.4

Lemma E.4

(Total variance lemma)
.

With probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{h=1}^{H}[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}R_{h}+{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})\leq 2{\mathcal{V}}^{2}K+2H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\log\frac{1}{\delta}.

Proof of Lemma
E.4
.

The proof uses a similar argument as Lemma C.5 in
[
Jin et al. 2018
]
.
Notice that the first state
s_{1,k}
is fixed and
a_{h,k}=\pi_{h}^{k}(s_{h,k})
.
Therefore,
(s_{2,k},\cdots,s_{H,k})
is a sequence generated by following policy
\pi_{k}
starting at
s_{1,k}
.
Let
{\mathcal{G}}_{k}
be the
\sigma
-field generated by all the random variables over the first
k
episodes.

X_{k}=\sum_{h=1}^{H}[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}R_{h}+{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})
.
We have the following properties about
X_{k}
.
Clearly
\pi_{k}
is
{\mathcal{G}}_{k-1}
-measurable,
X_{k}\geq 0
is
{\mathcal{G}}_{k}
-measurable, and
|X_{k}|\leq H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})
.

Let
{\mathbb{E}}_{k}(\cdot):={\mathbb{E}}[\cdot|{\mathcal{G}}_{k}]
for simplicity.

\displaystyle{\mathcal{V}}^{2}

\displaystyle\geq{\mathbb{E}}_{k-1}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}R_{h}(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})-V_{1}^{\pi_{k}}(s_{1,k})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}{\mathbb{E}}_{k-1}\left[\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(R_{h}(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})+V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}(s_{{h+1},k})-V_{h}^{\pi_{k}}(s_{h,k})\right)\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{=}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{\mathbb{E}}_{k-1}\left[R_{h}(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})+V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}(s_{{h+1},k})-V_{h}^{\pi_{k}}(s_{h,k})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{=}\sum_{h=1}^{H}{\mathbb{E}}_{k-1}\left[[R_{h}-r_{h}]^{2}(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})+\left[r_{h}(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})+V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}(s_{{h+1},k})-V_{h}^{\pi_{k}}(s_{h,k})\right]^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(d)}{=}{\mathbb{E}}_{k-1}\sum_{h=1}^{H}[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}R_{h}+{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})={\mathbb{E}}[X_{k}|{\mathcal{F}}_{k-1}]

where
(a)
uses
V_{H+1}^{\pi_{k}}(\cdot)=0
,
(b)
uses the independence due to the Markov
property,
(c)
holds since
R_{h}(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})
is independent with
s_{h+1,k}
conditioning on
(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})
, and
(d)
uses
V_{h}^{\pi_{k}}(s_{h,k})=r_{h}(s_{h,k},a_{h,k})+{\mathbb{E}}_{s_{h+1,k}\sim{\mathbb{P}}_{h}(\cdot|s_{h,k},a_{h,k})}[V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}(s_{h+1,k})]
.
Using
\Var[X_{k}|{\mathcal{G}}_{k-1}]\leq H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\cdot{\mathbb{E}}[X_{k}|{\mathcal{G}}_{k-1}]
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Var[X_{k}|{\mathcal{G}}_{k-1}]\leq H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\cdot\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}[X_{k}|{\mathcal{G}}_{k-1}]\leq(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2}){\mathcal{V}}^{2}HK.

By the Freedman inequality in Lemma
F.1
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}

\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}[{\mathbb{V}}_{h}R_{h}+{\mathbb{V}}_{h}V_{h+1}^{\pi_{k}}](s_{h,k},a_{h,k})

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}X_{k}\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}[X_{k}|{\mathcal{F}}_{k-1}]+\sqrt{2(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2}){\mathcal{V}}^{2}HK\log\frac{1}{\delta}}+\frac{2}{3}H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\log\frac{1}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq{\mathcal{V}}^{2}K+2\sqrt{{\mathcal{V}}^{2}K\cdot H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\log\frac{1}{\delta}}+\frac{2}{3}H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\log\frac{1}{\delta}

\displaystyle\leq 2{\mathcal{V}}^{2}K+2H(\sigma_{R}^{2}+{\mathcal{H}}^{2})\log\frac{1}{\delta}.

∎

Appendix F
Auxiliary Lemmas

F.1
Concentration Inequalities

Lemma F.1

(Freedman inequality
[
Freedman 1975
]
)
.

Let
\{X_{t}\}_{t\in[T]}
be a stochastic process that adapts to the filtration
{\mathcal{F}}_{t}
so that
X_{t}
is
{\mathcal{F}}_{t}
-measurable,
{\mathbb{E}}[X_{t}|{\mathcal{F}}_{t-1}]=0
,
|X_{t}|\leq M
and
\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}[X_{t}^{2}|{\mathcal{F}}_{t-1}]\leq V
where
M>0
and
V>0
are positive constants.
Then with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\sum_{t=1}^{T}X_{t}\leq\sqrt{2V\ln\frac{1}{\delta}}+\frac{2M}{3}\ln\frac{1}{\delta}.

Lemma F.2

(Variance-aware Freedman inequality)
.

Let
\{X_{t}\}_{t\in[T]}
be a stochastic process that adapts to the filtration
{\mathcal{F}}_{t}
so that
X_{t}
is
{\mathcal{F}}_{t}
-measurable,
{\mathbb{E}}[X_{t}|{\mathcal{F}}_{t-1}]=0
,
|X_{t}|\leq M
and
\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}[X_{t}^{2}|{\mathcal{F}}_{t-1}]\leq V^{2}
where
M>0
and
V>0
are positive constants.
Then with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have

\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}X_{t}\right|\leq 3\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}[X_{t}^{2}|{\mathcal{F}}_{t-1}]\cdot\log\frac{2K}{\delta}}+5M\log\frac{2K}{\delta}

where
K=1+\lceil 2\log_{2}\frac{V}{M}\rceil
.

Proof of Lemma
F.2
.

By Theorem 5 in
[
Li et al. 2021
]
, we have for any positive integer
K\geq 1
,

{\mathbb{P}}\left(\left|\sum_{t=1}^{T}X_{t}\right|\leq\sqrt{8\max\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}{\mathbb{E}}[X_{t}^{2}|{\mathcal{F}}_{t-1}],\frac{V^{2}}{2^{K}}\right\}\cdot\ln\frac{2K}{\delta}}+\frac{4M}{3}\ln\frac{2K}{\delta}\right)\geq 1-\delta.

By setting
K=1+\lceil 2\log_{2}\frac{V}{M}\rceil
, we have
\frac{V^{2}}{2^{K}}\leq M^{2}
.
Using
\max\{a,b\}\leq a+b
,
\sqrt{a+b}\leq\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}
for any
a,b\geq 0
and
\ln\frac{2K}{\delta}\geq 1
, we complete the proof.
∎

The following two lemmas are the counterpart lemmas of Theorem
3.1
under light-tail assumption.

Lemma F.3

(Bernstein inequality for self-normalized martingales, Lemma F.4 in
[
Hu et al. 2022
]
)
.

Let
\{{\mathcal{G}}_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
be a filtration and
\{{\bm{x}}_{t},\eta_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
be a stochastic process so that
{\bm{x}}_{t}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
is
{\mathcal{G}}_{t}
-measurable and
\eta_{t}\in{\mathbb{R}}
is
{\mathcal{G}}_{t+1}
-measurable..
If
\|{\bm{x}}_{t}\|\leq L
and
\{\eta_{t}\}_{t\geq 1}
satisfies that
{\mathbb{E}}[\eta_{t}|{\mathcal{G}}_{t}]=0
,
{\mathbb{E}}[\eta_{t}^{2}|{\mathcal{G}}_{t}]\leq\sigma^{2}
and
|\eta_{t}\min\left\{1,\|{\bm{x}}_{t}\|_{{\bm{Z}}_{t-1}^{-1}}\right\}|\leq M
for all
t\geq 1
.
Then, for any
\delta\in(0,1)
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have for all
t\geq 1
,

\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\bm{x}}_{j}\eta_{j}\right\|_{{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{-1}}\leq 8\sigma\sqrt{d\log\left(1+\frac{tL^{2}}{d\lambda}\right)\log\frac{4t^{2}}{\delta}}+4M\log\frac{4t^{2}}{\delta}

where
{\bm{Z}}_{t}=\lambda{\bm{I}}+\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\bm{x}}_{j}{\bm{x}}_{j}^{\top}
for
t\geq 1
and
{\bm{Z}}_{0}=\lambda{\bm{I}}
.

Lemma F.4

(Hoeffding inequality for self-normalized martingales, Theorem 1 in
[
Abbasi-Yadkori et al. 2011
]
)
.

Let
\{{\mathcal{G}}_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
be a filtration and
\{{\bm{x}}_{t},\eta_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
be a stochastic process so that
{\bm{x}}_{t}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
is
{\mathcal{G}}_{t}
-measurable and
\eta_{t}\in{\mathbb{R}}
is
{\mathcal{G}}_{t+1}
-measurable..
If
\|{\bm{x}}_{t}\|\leq L
and
\{\eta_{t}\}_{t\geq 1}
satisfies that
{\mathbb{E}}[\eta_{t}|{\mathcal{G}}_{t}]=0
and
|\eta_{t}|\leq M
for all
t\geq 1
.
Then, for any
\delta\in(0,1)
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, we have for all
t\geq 1
,

\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\bm{x}}_{j}\eta_{j}\right\|_{{\bm{Z}}_{t}^{-1}}\leq M\sqrt{d\log\left(1+\frac{tL^{2}}{d\lambda}\right)+\log\frac{1}{\delta}}

where
{\bm{Z}}_{t}=\lambda{\bm{I}}+\sum_{j=1}^{t}{\bm{x}}_{j}{\bm{x}}_{j}^{\top}
for
t\geq 1
and
{\bm{Z}}_{0}=\lambda{\bm{I}}
.

F.2
Elliptical Lemmas

Lemma F.5

(Lemma 11 in
[
Abbasi-Yadkori et al. 2011
]
)
.

Let
\{{\bm{x}}_{t}\}_{t\geq 1}\subset{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
and assume
\|{\bm{x}}_{t}\|\leq L
for all
t\geq 1
.
Set
{\bm{Z}}_{t}=\sum_{s=1}^{t}{\bm{x}}_{t}{\bm{x}}_{t}^{\top}+\lambda{\bm{I}}
.
Then it follows that

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\min\left\{1,\|{\bm{x}}_{t}\|^{2}_{{\bm{Z}}_{t-1}}\right\}\leq 2d\log\left(\frac{d\lambda+TL^{2}}{d\lambda}\right).

Lemma F.6

(Lemma 12 in
[
Abbasi-Yadkori et al. 2011
]
)
.

Suppose
{\bm{A}},{\bm{B}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d\times d}
are two positive definite matrices satisfying that
{\bm{A}}\succeq{\bm{B}}
, then for any
{\bm{x}}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}
,

\|{\bm{x}}\|_{{\bm{B}}^{-1}}\leq\|{\bm{x}}\|_{{\bm{A}}^{-1}}\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{det}({\bm{A}})}{\mathrm{det}({\bm{B}})}}.

F.3
Function Class and Covering Number

This subsection collects important lemmas in
[
He et al. 2022
]
.
Let
{\mathcal{K}}=\left\{k_{1},k_{2},\cdots\right\}
denote the set of episodes where the algorithm updates the value function in Algorithm
1
.
For a given total number of episodes
K
, it definitely follows that
|{\mathcal{K}}|\leq K
.
Furthermore, due to the mechanism of rare-switching value function updates,
|{\mathcal{K}}|
is actually much smaller than
K
.

Lemma F.7
.

|{\mathcal{K}}|\leq dH\log_{2}\left(1+\frac{K}{\lambda\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right).

Proof of Lemma
F.7
.

The proof is almost identical to Lemma E.1 in
[
He et al. 2022
]
except that we maintain the dependence on
\sigma_{\min}
.
According to the determinant-based criterion, for each episode
k_{i}
, there exists a stage
h^{\prime}\in[H]
such that
\mathrm{det}({\bm{H}}_{h^{\prime},k_{i}-1})\geq 2\mathrm{det}({\bm{H}}_{h^{\prime},k_{i-1}-1})
.
Since we always have
{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{i}-1}\succeq{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{i-1}-1}
for all
h\in[H]
, it then follows that

\prod_{h\in[H]}\mathrm{det}({\bm{H}}_{h,k_{i}-1})\geq 2\prod_{h\in[H]}\mathrm{det}({\bm{H}}_{h,k_{i-1}-1}).

By induction, it follows that

\prod_{h\in[H]}\mathrm{det}({\bm{H}}_{h,k_{|{\mathcal{K}}|}-1})\geq 2^{|{\mathcal{K}}|}\prod_{h\in[H]}\mathrm{det}({\bm{H}}_{h,k_{1}-1})\geq 2^{|{\mathcal{K}}|}\prod_{h\in[H]}\mathrm{det}(\lambda{\bm{I}})=2^{|{\mathcal{K}}|}\lambda^{dH}

On the other hand, due to
{\bm{H}}_{h,k_{|{\mathcal{K}}|}-1}\preceq{\bm{H}}_{h,K}
the determinant
\mathrm{det}({\bm{H}}_{h,k_{|{\mathcal{K}}|}-1}
is upper bounded by

\prod_{h\in[H]}\mathrm{det}({\bm{H}}_{h,k_{|{\mathcal{K}}|}-1})\leq\prod_{h\in[H]}\mathrm{det}({\bm{H}}_{h,K})\leq\left(\lambda+\frac{K}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right)^{dH}.

Combining the last two inequalities, we have

|{\mathcal{K}}|\leq dH\log_{2}\left(1+\frac{K}{\lambda\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\right).

∎

The optimistic value function
\overline{V}_{h}^{k}(\cdot)=\min_{k_{i}\leq k}\max_{a}\overline{Q}_{h}^{k_{i}}(\cdot,a)
belong to the function class
{\mathcal{V}}^{+}

{\mathcal{V}}^{+}=\left\{f|f(\cdot)=\max_{a\in{\mathcal{A}}}\min_{i\leq|{\mathcal{K}}|}\min\left\{{\bm{w}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{\phi}}(\cdot,a)+\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}(\cdot,a)\|_{{\bm{H}}_{i}^{-1}},{\mathcal{H}}\right\},\beta\in[0,B],\|{\bm{w}}_{i}\|\leq L,{\bm{H}}_{i}\succeq\lambda{\bm{I}}\right\}.

(F.1)

while the pessimistic value function
\underline{V}_{h}^{k}(\cdot)=\max_{k_{i}\leq k}\max_{a}\underline{Q}_{h}^{k_{i}}(\cdot,a)
belong to the function class
{\mathcal{V}}^{-}
,

{\mathcal{V}}^{-}=\left\{f|f(\cdot)=\max_{a\in{\mathcal{A}}}\max_{i\leq|{\mathcal{K}}|}\max\left\{{\bm{w}}_{i}^{\top}{\bm{\phi}}(\cdot,a)-\beta\|{\bm{\phi}}(\cdot,a)\|_{{\bm{H}}_{i}^{-1}},{\mathcal{H}}\right\},\beta\in[0,B],\|{\bm{w}}_{i}\|\leq L,{\bm{H}}_{i}\succeq\lambda{\bm{I}}\right\}.

(F.2)

Here
B
upper bounds
\beta
and
L=W+{\mathcal{H}}\sqrt{\frac{dK}{\lambda}}
is a uniformly bound for
{{\bm{\theta}}}_{h,k-1}+{\bm{\mu}}_{h,k-1}\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k}
because

\|{{\bm{\theta}}}_{h,k-1}+{\bm{\mu}}_{h,k-1}\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k}\|\leq\|{{\bm{\theta}}}_{h,k-1}\|+\|{\bm{\mu}}_{h,k-1}\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k}\|\leq W+{\mathcal{H}}\sqrt{\frac{dK}{\lambda}}

where the last inequality uses the boundedness of
{\bm{\theta}}_{h,k-1}
’s and the inequality
\|{\bm{\mu}}_{h,k-1}\overline{{\bm{V}}}_{h+1}^{k}\|\leq{\mathcal{H}}\sqrt{\frac{dK}{\lambda}}
(whose proof can be found in Lemma E.2 of
He et al. 2022
).

Lemma F.8

(Covering number of value functions)
.

Let
{\mathcal{V}}^{\pm}
denote the class of optimistic or pessimistic value functions with definition in (
F.1
) and (
F.2
) respectively.
Assume
\|{\bm{\phi}}(s,a)\|\leq 1
for all
(s,a)
pairs, and let
{\mathcal{N}}({\mathcal{V}},\varepsilon)
be the
\varepsilon
-covering number of
{\mathcal{V}}
with respective to the distance
\mathrm{dist}(f,f^{\prime}):=\sup_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}|f(s)-f^{\prime}(s)|
.
Then,

\log{\mathcal{N}}({\mathcal{V}}^{\pm},\varepsilon)\leq\left[d\log\left(1+\frac{4L}{\varepsilon}\right)+d^{2}\log\left(1+\frac{8d^{1/2}B^{2}}{\lambda\varepsilon^{2}}\right)\right]\cdot|{\mathcal{K}}|.

Proof of Lemma
F.8
.

The result about
{\mathcal{V}}_{f}^{+}
follows from Lemma E.6 in
[
He et al. 2022
]
.
The result about
{\mathcal{V}}_{f}^{-}
follows from Lemma E.7 in
[
He et al. 2022
]
.
∎

Lemma F.9

(Covering number of squared functions, Lemma E.8 in
[
He et al. 2022
]
)
.

For the squared function class
[{\mathcal{V}}^{+}]^{2}:=\left\{f^{2}|f\in{\mathcal{V}}^{+}\right\}
, let
{\mathcal{N}}([{\mathcal{V}}^{+}]^{2},\varepsilon)
be the
\varepsilon
-covering number of
[{\mathcal{V}}^{+}]^{2}
with respective to the distance
\mathrm{dist}(f,f^{\prime}):=\sup_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}|f(s)-f^{\prime}(s)|
.
Then

\log{\mathcal{N}}([{\mathcal{V}}^{+}]^{2},\varepsilon)\leq\left[d\log\left(1+\frac{8HL}{\varepsilon}\right)+d^{2}\log\left(1+\frac{32d^{1/2}H^{2}B^{2}}{\lambda\varepsilon^{2}}\right)\right]\cdot|{\mathcal{K}}|.
